Elements to remove: linesegarray, charShapeIdRef, and similar layout caches\n    to_remove = []\n    for child in paragraph:\n        tag = child.tag\n        if tag.startswith('{' + hp_ns + '}'):\n            local_name = tag[len('{' + hp_ns + '}'):]\n            # Check if this is a paragraph element - track it for layout cache removal\n    is_paragraph = element.tag == get_full_tag(hp_ns, 'p')\n    paragraph_modified = False\n\n    # Handle text content in hp:t elements\n    if element.tag == get_full_tag(hp_ns, 't'):\n        if element.text:\n            original_text = element.text\n            new_text = original_text\n            # Replace all placeholders\n            for key, value in data.items():\n                placeholder = '{{' + key + '}}'\n                if placeholder in new_text:\n                    new_text = new_text.replace(placeholder, str(value))\n\n            if new_text != original_text:\n                element.text = new_text\n                print(f\"  Replaced: '{original_text}' -> '{new_text}'\")\n                return True\n        return False\n\n    # Process children\n    for child in element:\n        if replace_in_element(child, data, modified_paragraphs):\n            paragraph_modified = True\n\n    # If this is a paragraph and was modified, mark it\n    if is_paragraph and paragraph_modified:\n        modified_paragraphs.add(element)\n\n    return paragraph_modified\n\ndef process_xml_file(filepath):\n    \"\"\"Process an XML file, replacing placeholders.\"\"\"\n    print(f\"\\nProcessing: {filepath}\")\n\n    # Parse the XML\n    tree = ET.parse(filepath)\n    root = tree.getroot()\n\n    # Track modified paragraphs for layout cache removal\n    modified_paragraphs = set()\n\n    # Replace placeholders\n    replace_in_element(root, data, modified_paragraphs)\n\n    # Remove layout cache from modified paragraphs\n    for para in modified_paragraphs:\n        remove_layout_cache(para)\n\n    # Check if any placeholders remain\n    xml_str = ET.tostring(root, encoding='unicode')\n    remaining = re.findall(r'\\{\\{[^}]+\\}\\}', xml_str)\n    if remaining:\n        print(f\"  Warning: Remaining placeholders: {remaining}\")\n    else:\n        print(f\"  All placeholders replaced successfully\")\n\n    # Write back preserving XML declaration\n    # Get the original XML declaration\n    with open(filepath, 'rb') as f:\n        original_content = f.read().decode('utf-8')\n\n    # Extract XML declaration if present\n    xml_decl_match = re.match(r'(<\\?xml[^?]*\\?>)', original_content)\n    xml_decl = xml_decl_match.group(1) if xml_decl_match else '<?xml version=\"1.0\" encoding=\"UTF-8\"?>'\n\n    # Write the modified XML\n    tree.write(filepath, encoding='utf-8', xml_declaration=False)\n\n    # Prepend XML declaration\n    with open(filepath, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n        content = f.read()\n\n    with open(filepath, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n        f.write(xml_decl + '\\n' + content)\n\n    print(f\"  Written: {filepath}\")\n\n# Process all XML files in the template directory\nfor root_dir, dirs, files in os.walk(TEMPLATE_DIR):\n    for file in files:\n        if file.endswith('.xml'):\n            filepath = os.path.join(root_dir, file)\n            process_xml_file(filepath)\n\n# Re-pack into HWPX file\nprint(f\"\\nRe-packing to: {OUTPUT_FILE}\")\n\nwith zipfile.ZipFile(OUTPUT_FILE, 'w', zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as zf:\n    for root_dir, dirs, files in os.walk(TEMPLATE_DIR):\n        for file in files:\n            filepath = os.path.join(root_dir, file)\n            arcname = os.path.relpath(filepath, TEMPLATE_DIR)\n            zf.write(filepath, arcname)\n            print(f\"  Added: {arcname}\")\n\nprint(f\"\\nDone!
